# Deutsch (de): reviewed translations

Every entry of `locale/de/LC_MESSAGES/strings.po` that this change replaces. "Old" is the translation in the repository before this change (marked *fuzzy* when the program did not use it), "New" the reviewed one. Approve or correct each row in `de.csv` (columns *Approved* and *Translator note*). See [README.md](README.md) for how the changes were made.

| Type | Count |
|---|---|
| format | 8 |
| corrected | 965 |
| fuzzy corrected | 260 |
| fuzzy confirmed | 8 |
| untranslated copy | 14 |
| new | 229 |
| manual | 2 |
| **Total** | **1486** |

| 38 | corrected | How much (percentage) of the tool width to overlap each tool pass.<br>Adjust the value starting with lower values<br>and increasing it if areas that should be processed are still <br>not processed.<br>Lower values = faster processing, faster execution on CNC.<br>Higher values = slow processing and slow execution on CNC<br>due of too many paths. | Wie viel (Prozent) der Werkzeugbreite, um jeden Werkzeugdurchlauf zu überlappen.<br>Passen Sie den Wert beginnend mit niedrigeren Werten an<br>und es zu erhöhen, wenn Bereiche, die verarbeitet werden sollen, noch vorhanden sind<br>nicht verarbeitet.<br>Niedrigere Werte = schnellere Verarbeitung, schnellere Ausführung auf CNC.<br>Höhere Werte = langsame Verarbeitung und langsame Ausführung auf CNC<br>wegen zu vieler Wege. | Um wie viel Prozent der Werkzeugbreite sich die Werkzeugbahnen überlappen.<br>Beginnen Sie mit niedrigen Werten und erhöhen Sie<br>den Wert, wenn Bereiche, die bearbeitet werden sollen, noch<br>nicht bearbeitet sind.<br>Niedrigere Werte = schnellere Berechnung, schnellere Ausführung auf der CNC.<br>Höhere Werte = langsame Berechnung und langsame Ausführung auf der CNC<br>wegen zu vieler Bahnen. | Broken grammar (word-for-word machine translation). | ☐ |

| 348 | corrected | Shortcuts List | Tastenkürzel Liste | Tastenkürzel-Liste | Spelling: German compounds must not be split by a space. | ☐ |
| 349 | corrected | YouTube Channel | Youtube Kanal | YouTube-Kanal | Spelling: brand name is 'YouTube', and the compound needs a hyphen. | ☐ |
| 350 | new | Donate | — | Spenden | New translation (Help menu, PayPal donation). | ☐ |
| 351 | corrected | How To | How To | Anleitung | English 'How To' had been left untranslated in the Help menu item and dialog title. | ☐ |
| 352 | corrected | Union | Union | Vereinigung | Boolean geometry union; English 'Union' was left untranslated. The German term is 'Vereinigung'. | ☐ |
| 353 | corrected | Alt Subtraction | Alt-Subtraktion | Alternative Subtraktion | 'Alt' here means 'alternative'. In German 'Alt-Subtraktion' reads as 'old subtraction' or as the Alt key. | ☐ |
| 354 | corrected | Transform | Verwandeln | Transformieren | 'Verwandeln' means 'to magically change'. The CAD term is 'Transformieren' (glossary: Transformation Tool = Transformationswerkzeug). | ☐ |
| 355 | corrected | Enable Plot | Diagramm aktivieren | Darstellung aktivieren | 'Plot' here means drawing the object on the canvas; 'Diagramm' (chart) is the wrong meaning. | ☐ |
| 356 | corrected | Disable Plot | Diagramm deaktivieren | Darstellung deaktivieren | 'Plot' means the on-canvas drawing of the object, not a chart ('Diagramm'). | ☐ |
| 357 | corrected | Set Color | Farbsatz | Farbe festlegen | 'Farbsatz' means 'color set/palette'; the menu sets the object's color. | ☐ |
| 358 | corrected | Red | Rote | Rot | Grammar: the color name is 'Rot', not the inflected adjective 'Rote' (matches 'Blau', 'Gelb'). | ☐ |
| 359 | new | Indigo | — | Indigo | Color name; 'Indigo' is the German word as well. | ☐ |
| 360 | corrected | Edit Toolbar | Symbolleiste bearbeiten | Bearbeiten-Symbolleiste | Wrong meaning: 'Symbolleiste bearbeiten' means 'edit the toolbar'; this is the name of the Edit toolbar. | ☐ |
| 361 | corrected | View Toolbar | Symbolleiste anzeigen | Ansicht-Symbolleiste | Wrong meaning: 'Symbolleiste anzeigen' means 'show toolbar'; this is the name of the View toolbar. | ☐ |
| 362 | fuzzy corrected | Plugin Toolbar | *fuzzy:* Dateisymbolleiste | Plugin-Symbolleiste | Fuzzy text said 'File toolbar', the wrong toolbar; this is the Plugin toolbar. | ☐ |
| 363 | corrected | Excellon Editor Toolbar | Excellon Editor-Symbolleiste | Excellon-Editor-Symbolleiste | Spelling: German compounds need hyphens all the way through (Excellon-Editor-Symbolleiste). | ☐ |
| 364 | corrected | Geometry Editor Toolbar | Geometrie Editor-Symbolleiste | Geometrie-Editor-Symbolleiste | Spelling: German compounds need hyphens all the way through. | ☐ |
| 365 | corrected | Gerber Editor Toolbar | Gerber Editor-Symbolleiste | Gerber-Editor-Symbolleiste | Spelling: German compounds need hyphens all the way through. | ☐ |
| 366 | new | Open a Gerber file. | — | Eine Gerber-Datei öffnen. | New translation (toolbar tooltip). | ☐ |
| 367 | new | Open a Excellon file. | — | Eine Excellon-Datei öffnen. | New translation (toolbar tooltip). | ☐ |
| 368 | new | Copy a selection of objects. | — | Die ausgewählten Objekte kopieren. | New translation (toolbar tooltip). | ☐ |
| 369 | new | Delete a selection of objects. | — | Die ausgewählten Objekte löschen. | New translation (toolbar tooltip). | ☐ |
| 370 | new | Measure a distance between two or more points. | — | Den Abstand zwischen zwei oder mehr Punkten messen. | New translation (Measure tool tooltip). | ☐ |
| 371 | new | Set an origin to a custom location. | — | Den Ursprung auf eine frei wählbare Position setzen. | New translation; 'Ursprung' for origin as in the glossary. | ☐ |
| 372 | new | Jump to | — | Springen zu | New translation, matches glossary 'Jump to ...' = 'Springen zu ...'. | ☐ |
| 373 | new | Move the mouse cursor to a defined position and center into view. | — | Den Mauszeiger an eine festgelegte Position bewegen und die Ansicht darauf zentrieren. | New translation (Jump to tooltip). | ☐ |
| 374 | new | Locate | — | Lokalisieren | New translation, matches glossary 'Locate ...' = 'Lokalisieren ...'. | ☐ |
| 375 | new | Locate a predefined position on a selected object. | — | Eine vordefinierte Position auf einem ausgewählten Objekt lokalisieren. | New translation (Locate tooltip). | ☐ |
| 376 | corrected | Replot | Replotieren | Neu zeichnen | 'Replotieren' is not a German word; the button redraws all objects. | ☐ |
| 377 | corrected | Clear Plot | Plot klar löschen | Zeichenfläche leeren | 'Plot klar löschen' makes no sense (literal 'clear' = 'klar'); the action clears the canvas. | ☐ |
| 378 | corrected | Panel | Platte | Panel | Wrong meaning: 'Platte' means a plate or slab. This toolbar button opens the Panelize plugin, and the glossary uses 'Panel'. | ☐ |
| 379 | fuzzy corrected | Film | *fuzzy:* Film PCB | Film | Fuzzy 'Film PCB' added a word that is not in the source. The button label is just 'Film'. | ☐ |
| 380 | new | Align | — | Ausrichten | New translation (Align plugin toolbar button). | ☐ |
| 381 | corrected | Thieving | Diebstahl | Kupferausgleich | Mistranslated PCB term: 'Diebstahl' means theft. Copper thieving means extra copper areas added to even out plating, in German 'Kupferausgleich(sflächen)'. | ☐ |
| 382 | fuzzy corrected | Markers | *fuzzy:* Kennzeichen | Marker | Fuzzy 'Kennzeichen' means label/licence plate, which is wrong. This is the corner Markers plugin; 'Marker' matches the glossary ('Add Marker' = 'Marker hinzufügen'). | ☐ |
| 383 | new | Punch | — | Stanzen | New translation. Punch Gerber plugin (punching holes in pads); matches glossary 'Löcher stanzen'. | ☐ |
| 384 | corrected | Select | Wählen | Auswählen | Selection tool. 'Wählen' means choose or dial; made consistent with 'Abbrechen und zurück zu Auswählen' (id 1150). | ☐ |
| 385 | corrected | Add Drill | Bohrer hinzufügen | Bohrung hinzufügen | Domain term: the Excellon editor adds a drill hole ('Bohrung'), not a drill bit ('Bohrer'). | ☐ |
| 386 | corrected | Add Drill Array | Bohrer-Array hinzufügen | Bohrungs-Array hinzufügen | Domain term: an array of drill holes ('Bohrungen'), not of drill bits ('Bohrer'). | ☐ |
| 387 | corrected | Add Slot | Steckplatz hinzufügen | Schlitz hinzufügen | Mistranslated: 'Steckplatz' is an expansion slot. In Excellon a slot is a milled oblong hole, 'Schlitz' as in the glossary. | ☐ |
| 388 | corrected | Add Slot Array | Steckplatz-Array hinzufügen | Schlitz-Array hinzufügen | Mistranslated: 'Steckplatz' is an expansion slot; the Excellon slot is a 'Schlitz'. | ☐ |
| 389 | new | Explode | — | Zerlegen | New translation. Geometry editor 'Explode' splits polygons into line segments; 'Zerlegen' is the usual German CAD term. | ☐ |
| 390 | new | Polygon Subtraction. First selected is the target.<br>The rest of the selected is subtracted from the first.<br>First selected is replaced by the result. | — | Polygon-Subtraktion. Das zuerst ausgewählte Element ist das Ziel.<br>Die übrigen ausgewählten Elemente werden vom ersten abgezogen.<br>Das zuerst ausgewählte Element wird durch das Ergebnis ersetzt. | New translation of the tooltip; keeps the 3 lines. | ☐ |
| 391 | new | Alt Subtraction. First selected is the target.<br>The rest of the selected is subtracted from the first.<br>First selected is kept besides the result. | — | Alt-Subtraktion. Das zuerst ausgewählte Element ist das Ziel.<br>Die übrigen ausgewählten Elemente werden vom ersten abgezogen.<br>Das zuerst ausgewählte Element bleibt neben dem Ergebnis erhalten. | New translation of the tooltip; 'Alt-Subtraktion' as in the glossary; keeps the 3 lines. | ☐ |
| 392 | corrected | When active, value on Grid_X<br>is copied to the Grid_Y value. | Wenn aktiv, Wert auf Grid_X<br>wird in den Wert von Grid_Y kopiert. | Wenn aktiv, wird der Wert von Grid_X<br>in den Wert von Grid_Y kopiert. | Broken word order and grammar ('Wenn aktiv, Wert auf Grid_X wird ...'). | ☐ |
| 393 | corrected | Snap to corner | In der Ecke ausrichten | An Ecken ausrichten | Wrong meaning: 'In der Ecke ausrichten' means 'align in the corner'. The option snaps to corners; matches 'Am Raster ausrichten'. | ☐ |
| 394 | untranslated copy | TCL Shell | TCL Shell | TCL-Shell | Tcl and Shell are kept. Only the German compound hyphen is added, as in 'Shell-Symbolleiste'. | ☐ |
| 395 | corrected | Plot Area | Grundstücksfläche | Zeichenbereich | Wrong meaning: 'Grundstücksfläche' is a plot of land. This is the drawing area (canvas), as in 'Zeichenbereich umschalten'. | ☐ |
| 396 | corrected | CNC-JOB | CNC-Auftrag | CNC-JOB | Preferences tab title. The other tabs are uppercased (GERBER, GEOMETRIE ...), so the translation should be uppercase too. — reviewer: Uppercase is right, because the other tabs are .upper() of the translated name. But this tab holds the CNCJob object preferences, and the German UI keeps the object type name 'CNCJob' unchanged everywhere (e.g. 'CNCJob-Objekte ...'). So the name is kept instead of 'AUFTRAG'. | ☐ |
| 397 | new | Engraving | — | Gravur | New translation. Preferences tab for the engraving plugins (shown in upper case). | ☐ |
| 398 | fuzzy corrected | Processing | *fuzzy:* Analysieren | Bearbeitung | Fuzzy 'Analysieren' (analyse) is wrong. This preferences tab holds the machining plugins (drilling, milling, isolation...), so it is 'Bearbeitung'. | ☐ |
| 399 | fuzzy corrected | Extra Plugins | *fuzzy:* plugin_tab | Weitere Plugins | Fuzzy 'plugin_tab' was an internal identifier, not a translation. | ☐ |
| 400 | corrected | UTILITIES | NUTZEN | DIENSTPROGRAMME | Wrong meaning: 'NUTZEN' means 'benefit' (and a PCB panel). This preferences tab holds the utilities ('Dienstprogramme', as in the glossary). | ☐ |

B. Layout, GUI-Zustand, Stil usw. | Fuzzy text added 'HDPI-Unterstützung', which is not in the source, and broke the sentence with a period. | ☐ |

'Union' is a polygon union ('Vereinigung'). | ☐ |

| 418 | corrected | Shortcut Key List | Tastenkürzel Liste | Tastenkürzelliste | Spelling: a compound noun is written as one word. | ☐ |
| 419 | corrected | General Shortcut list | Tastenkürzel Liste | Allgemeine Tastenkürzel | The word 'General' was missing, so the title could not be told apart from the editor shortcut list. | ☐ |

| 421 | fuzzy corrected | Open Properties Plugin | *fuzzy:* Öffnen Sie das Eigenschaften-Tool | Eigenschaften-Plugin öffnen | Fuzzy text used the polite imperative and 'Tool'. Shortcut list entries use the infinitive style ('Objekt kopieren'), and the source says Plugin. | ☐ |

| 429 | corrected | Non-Copper Clearing | Nicht-Kupfer-Clearing | Restkupfer entfernen (NCC) | English left untranslated ('Clearing'). Non-copper clearing means removing the unused copper; NCC is kept as the abbreviation used elsewhere. | ☐ |

| 431 | fuzzy corrected | QRCode | *fuzzy:* Code | QR-Code | Fuzzy 'Code' dropped 'QR'. 'QR-Code' is the German spelling (plugin tab title). | ☐ |
| 432 | fuzzy corrected | Rules Check | *fuzzy:* Führen Sie die Regelprüfung durch | Regelprüfung | Fuzzy text was an imperative sentence ('Run the rules check'), but this is the plugin name in the shortcut list. | ☐ |

| 434 | fuzzy corrected | Subtract | *fuzzy:* Subtraktor | Subtrahieren | Fuzzy 'Subtraktor' means 'subtractor'. 'Subtract' is the plugin name, so the verb fits. | ☐ |
| 435 | corrected | Cutout PCB | Ausschnitt PCB | PCB ausschneiden | 'Ausschnitt PCB' is ungrammatical. The Cutout plugin cuts the board out. | ☐ |
| 436 | corrected | Panelize PCB | Panelisierung PCB | PCB panelisieren | 'Panelisierung PCB' is ungrammatical; the action panelizes the board. | ☐ |

Konvertiert einen Windows-Pfadstil in den in Tcl Shell erforderlichen | Inhalte einfügen. Konvertiert einen Windows-Pfad in das in der Tcl-Shell erforderliche Format | 'Paste Special' was left in English (the standard German term is 'Inhalte einfügen'), and the sentence was incomplete ('in den ... erforderlichen'). | ☐ |
| 438 | corrected | Editor Shortcut list | Editor-Verknüpfungsliste | Editor-Tastenkürzel | 'Verknüpfung' is a file link, not a keyboard shortcut; made consistent with the other shortcut list titles. | ☐ |
| 439 | corrected | Within Add Arc will toogle the ARC direction: CW or CCW | Innerhalb von Bogen hinzufügen wird die ARC-Richtung getippt: CW oder CCW | Beim Zeichnen eines Bogens wird die Bogenrichtung umgeschaltet: CW oder CCW | 'getippt' (typed) is wrong for 'toggle' ('umgeschaltet'), and 'Innerhalb von Bogen hinzufügen' is ungrammatical. — reviewer: Right idea. But the quoted label "Bogen hinzufügen" does not exist in the German UI, where the tool is only called 'Bogen'. Rephrased as 'while drawing an arc'. CW/CCW kept. | ☐ |
| 440 | corrected | Jump to Location (x, y) | Zum Standort springen (x, y) | Zur Position springen (x, y) | 'Standort' means a geographic site. On the canvas this is a position. | ☐ |

Made consistent with id 1124. — reviewer: Same fix as id 1124: no quoted tool name that does not exist in the German UI. | ☐ |
| 442 | corrected | Add Text Tool | Textwerkzeug hinzufügen | Textwerkzeug | Wrong meaning: 'Textwerkzeug hinzufügen' means 'add the text tool'. This shortcut entry (T) opens it, as with the neighbouring 'Pufferwerkzeug'. | ☐ |

| 447 | corrected | Offset shape on Y axis | Versetzte Form auf der Y-Achse | Form auf der Y-Achse versetzen | Wrong meaning: 'Versetzte Form' means 'the shifted shape'. This is the action that offsets the shape. | ☐ |

Can have a value between 2 and 32.<br>Default value is 2.<br>After change, it will be applied at next App start. | — | Die Anzahl der Qthreads, die dem Programm zur Verfügung stehen.<br>Eine größere Zahl kann Aufgaben beschleunigen, aber<br>je nach Rechnergeschwindigkeit dazu führen, dass das Programm<br>nicht mehr reagiert. | 868 | corrected | Failed join. The Geometry objects are of different types.<br>At least one is MultiGeo type and the other is SingleGeo type.
